14:22:32,085 INFO  - # Set a preference for ambiguous libs to use *-64bit libs
14:22:32,085 INFO  - #     *-64bit libs on 32bit build environment are required for .Net build (e.g., coreclr, corefx)
14:22:32,085 INFO  - #     But when building 64bit target, they could make 'have choice' error with original libs in 64bit repository.
14:22:32,085 INFO  - #     e.g., libstdc++ and libstdc++-64bit provides same libs. It's ambiguous to choose appropriate one.
